# Вкладка «План» в Mini App — план реализации

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Третья вкладка Mini App «План»: правка плана текущего месяца с телефона — суммы статей с подсказками по истории, список поступлений и платежей, цель накоплений — с записью во вкладку месяца Google-таблицы и сообщением второму пользователю.

**Architecture:** `app/services/plan.py` читает вкладку месяца (статьи в A:M по паре «подкатегория + валюта», список и цель в блоке прогноза O:S по заголовкам), считает подсказки по листу fact и превращает правки в одну запись `values_batch_update`, проверив, что ячейки не поменялись после открытия. API: `GET /api/plan?month=YYYY-MM` и `POST /api/plan` (409 со свежим планом при конфликте). Фронтенд держит черновик правок в `App` и в `localStorage`, экран `PlanScreen` считает итог по черновику и сохраняет кнопкой Telegram.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.12, python-telegram-bot 22.8, Starlette 1.7, Pydantic 2, gspread, pandas; React 19.3, TypeScript 6.0 (strict), Vite 8.3, Tailwind 4.3, Vitest 5.0, Testing Library.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-11-plan-tab-design.md`

Этот документ — **этап 1** спеки (раздел 14): план текущего месяца. Этапы 2–4 (следующий месяц, «+ статья», советник) дописываются в этот же файл перед началом каждого — по коду, который сложится после этапа 1.

## Global Constraints

- Репозиторий публичный: в коде, тестах и образцах только условные суммы и названия; никаких реальных сумм, имён и ID.
- Секреты не читаем и не вводим: `.env` не открываем.
- Python: type hints на всех сигнатурах, black + ruff; перед коммитом `ruff check . && black --check . && pytest -q` (venv проекта).
- Фронтенд: функциональные компоненты и хуки, TypeScript strict, без `any` и без `!`-утверждений, Tailwind; перед коммитом в `webapp/`: `npm run lint && npm test && npm run build`.
- Месяц плана — `YYYY-MM`; допустимы текущий и следующий месяц по `ANALYTICS_TIMEZONE`, иначе 422 с полем `month`.
- Статья — пара (подкатегория, валюта), ключ `«подкатегория|ВАЛЮТА»`; пара, встречающаяся во вкладке дважды, только для чтения (`editable: false`).
- План во вкладке — D со знаком минус (и у расходов, и у доходов); в API и на экране — положительный. `0 ≤ plan ≤ 10¹²`, до двух знаков после запятой.
- Строка списка: название 1–40 символов, день 1–31, сумма не 0 (знак: «+» поступление, «−» платёж), валюта из курсов system!H. Цель: `0 ≤ amount ≤ 10¹²`, валюта из курсов.
- Блок прогноза ищем по текстам заголовков (`LIST_HEADER`, `GOAL_LABEL`, `TABLE_HEADER` из `app/services/day_budget.py`), не по номерам строк.
- Ошибки API: 422 `validation` с полями `month`, `rows`, `events`, `goal`, `changes`; 409 `plan_changed` с телом `{"error", "plan", "conflicts"}`; 503 `sheets_unavailable`.
- Сохранение: одна запись `values_batch_update` (`RAW`); в процессе одно сохранение за раз (`bot_data["plan_lock"]`).
- Уведомление: всем из `ALLOWED_USER_IDS`, кроме автора; не больше 10 строк правок, дальше «… и ещё N»; имя — `first_name` из initData, иначе `bot.get_chat`, иначе без имени.
- Черновик в `localStorage` по ключу `plan-draft:<userId>:<YYYY-MM>`; доступ к `localStorage` всегда в `try/catch`.
- Коммиты — conventional commits, в конце `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`; тело PR заканчивается `🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)`; мерж — `gh pr merge --merge --delete-branch`.

## Review Focus

1. **Строку вставили во вкладку руками, пока план открыт** — запись находит статью по паре и пишет в сдвинутую строку, а не по старому номеру (тест в Task 5).
2. **Валютные суммы с центами** — «650,5 USD» доходит до таблицы как `-650.5`, сумма со знаком у платежа — `"-650.5"` (тесты в Task 7 и Task 9).
3. **Двое сохраняют одновременно** — побеждает один, второй получает 409, в таблице одна запись (тест в Task 7).
4. **Черновик с прошлого раза, а план за это время поменяли** — при открытии экрана правки ложатся на свежий план, изменившиеся строки подсвечены (тест в Task 11).
5. **Кнопка Telegram остаётся после ухода с вкладки или при открытом окне правки** — `MainButton` прячется при уходе и не показывается поверх окна (тест в Task 11).

---

## Этап 1 — план текущего месяца

Ветка `feat/plan-tab-month` от `main` после слияния PR #33 (спека и этот план). Итог этапа: вкладка «План» в проде, правки сохраняются во вкладку месяца, второй пользователь получает сообщение.

### Task 1: План месяца из вкладки

**Files:**
- Create: `app/services/plan.py`
- Test: `tests/test_month_plan.py`

**Interfaces:**

- Produces: `article_key(subcategory: str, currency: str) -> str`; `EventCells = tuple[str | None, int | None, float | None, str | None]`; `Article(key, kind, group, subcategory, currency, plan: float, row: int, editable: bool = True)`; `Event(slot, row, name, day, amount, currency)` с `.cells() -> EventCells`; `Goal(row, amount, currency)`; `MonthPlan(articles: tuple[Article, ...], events: tuple[Event, ...] | None, goal: Goal | None)`; `read_plan(rows: list[list[Any]]) -> MonthPlan`.

`tests/test_month_plan.py`:

```python
"""Вкладка «План»: план месяца из вкладки, подсказки, правки, запись."""

from typing import Any

from app.services.plan import Event, Goal, read_plan

HEADER = ["Статья", "Категория", "Подкатегория", "План", "Факт", "Дельта", "Валюта"]
EVENTS = [("Аванс", 15, 3_000_000, "UZS"), ("Квартплата", 18, -500, "USD")]


def article(
    kind: str, group: str, sub: str, plan: float, currency: str = "UZS"
) -> list[Any]:
    """Строка статьи: D — план со знаком минус, G — валюта."""
    return [kind, group, sub, -plan, 0, plan, currency]


def month_tab(
    articles: list[list[Any]],
    events: list[tuple[str, int, float, str]] = EVENTS,
    goal: tuple[float, str] | None = (0, "UZS"),
) -> list[list[Any]]:
    """Вкладка месяца: статьи слева (A:G), блок прогноза справа (O:R) в тех же строках.

    Заголовок списка — строка 11, восемь строк списка — 12–19, цель — 20, таблица
    дней — с 21, как во вкладках таблицы.
    """
    rows: list[list[Any]] = [HEADER, *articles]
    rows += [[] for _ in range(30 - len(rows))]
    rows = [list(row) + [""] * (19 - len(row)) for row in rows]
    rows[10][14:19] = [
        "Поступления (+) и крупные платежи (−)",
        "День",
        "Сумма",
        "Валюта",
        "В сумах",
    ]
    for k, (name, day, amount, currency) in enumerate(events):
        rows[11 + k][14:18] = [name, day, amount, currency]
    if goal is not None:
        rows[19][14:18] = ["🎯 Отложить за месяц (цель накоплений)", "", *goal]
    rows[20][14:18] = ["Дата", "План", "Факт", "Разница"]
    return rows


def test_articles_are_pairs_of_subcategory_and_currency() -> None:
    plan = read_plan(
        month_tab(
            [
                article("Расходы", "🍔 ЕДА", "кофе", 1_500_000),
                article("Расходы", "🍔 ЕДА", "кофе", 20, "USD"),
                article("Доходы", "💰 ДОХОДЫ", "зарплата", 10_000_000),
            ]
        )
    )
    assert [(a.key, a.kind, a.group, a.plan, a.row) for a in plan.articles] == [
        ("кофе|UZS", "spend", "🍔 ЕДА", 1_500_000, 2),
        ("кофе|USD", "spend", "🍔 ЕДА", 20, 3),
        ("зарплата|UZS", "income", "💰 ДОХОДЫ", 10_000_000, 4),
    ]
    assert all(a.editable for a in plan.articles)


def test_header_totals_and_blank_rows_are_not_articles() -> None:
    rows = month_tab([article("Расходы", "🍔 ЕДА", "кафе", 100)])
    rows[2][7:9] = [-100, -40]  # итоговая строка: только суммы H и I
    assert [a.key for a in read_plan(rows).articles] == ["кафе|UZS"]


def test_empty_plan_cell_is_zero_and_currency_defaults_to_sums() -> None:
    rows = month_tab([["Расходы", "🍔 ЕДА", "кафе", "", "", "", ""]])
    [only] = read_plan(rows).articles
    assert (only.key, only.plan) == ("кафе|UZS", 0.0)


def test_a_pair_met_twice_is_read_only() -> None:
    rows = month_tab(
        [
            article("Расходы", "🍔 ЕДА", "кафе", 100),
            article("Расходы", "🍔 ЕДА", "кафе", 200),
        ]
    )
    assert [a.editable for a in read_plan(rows).articles] == [False, False]


def test_events_and_goal_come_from_the_forecast_block() -> None:
    plan = read_plan(month_tab([], goal=(2_000_000, "UZS")))
    assert plan.events is not None and len(plan.events) == 8
    assert plan.events[0] == Event(0, 12, "Аванс", 15, 3_000_000, "UZS")
    assert plan.events[1] == Event(1, 13, "Квартплата", 18, -500, "USD")
    assert plan.events[2] == Event(2, 14, None, None, None, None)
    assert plan.goal == Goal(20, 2_000_000, "UZS")


def test_without_the_forecast_block_events_and_goal_are_unknown() -> None:
    plan = read_plan([HEADER, article("Расходы", "🍔 ЕДА", "кафе", 100)])
    assert (plan.events, plan.goal) == (None, None)
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Чтение вкладки**

`app/services/plan.py`:

```python
"""Вкладка «План» Mini App: план месяца из вкладки, подсказки, правки.

Вкладка месяца: статьи в A:M (тип A, группа B, подкатегория C, план D со знаком
минус, валюта G), справа блок прогноза O:S — список «Поступления (+) и крупные
платежи (−)» и цель накоплений. Статья — пара (подкатегория, валюта): по ней
вкладка считает факт, по ней же находим строку, а не по номеру.
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
from typing import Any

from app.services.day_budget import COLUMN_O, GOAL_LABEL, LIST_HEADER, TABLE_HEADER

KINDS = {"Расходы": "spend", "Доходы": "income"}
MAX_EVENTS = 20  # строк списка; дальше — уже не список, а сломанная вкладка

EventCells = tuple[str | None, int | None, float | None, str | None]


def article_key(subcategory: str, currency: str) -> str:
    """«кофе|UZS» — ключ статьи в API и черновике."""
    return f"{subcategory}|{currency}"


def _text(value: Any) -> str:
    return "" if value is None else str(value).strip()


def _number(value: Any) -> float | None:
    if isinstance(value, int | float) and not isinstance(value, bool):
        return float(value)
    return None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Article:
    """Строка статьи: план положительный, в валюте строки; row — номер строки листа."""

    key: str
    kind: str  # spend | income
    group: str
    subcategory: str
    currency: str
    plan: float
    row: int
    editable: bool = True


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Event:
    """Строка списка поступлений и платежей; у пустой name — None."""

    slot: int
    row: int
    name: str | None
    day: int | None
    amount: float | None
    currency: str | None

    def cells(self) -> EventCells:
        return self.name, self.day, self.amount, self.currency


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Goal:
    row: int
    amount: float
    currency: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MonthPlan:
    articles: tuple[Article, ...]
    events: tuple[Event, ...] | None  # None — во вкладке нет блока прогноза
    goal: Goal | None


def _articles(rows: list[list[Any]]) -> tuple[Article, ...]:
    found: list[Article] = []
    for number, row in enumerate(rows[1:], start=2):
        cells = list(row[:7]) + [""] * (7 - len(row[:7]))
        kind = KINDS.get(_text(cells[0]))
        group, sub = _text(cells[1]), _text(cells[2])
        if kind is None or not group or not sub:
            continue
        currency = _text(cells[6]).upper() or "UZS"
        plan = 0.0 - (_number(cells[3]) or 0.0)
        key = article_key(sub, currency)
        found.append(Article(key, kind, group, sub, currency, plan, number))
    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    for article in found:
        counts[article.key] = counts.get(article.key, 0) + 1
    return tuple(
        replace(article, editable=False) if counts[article.key] > 1 else article
        for article in found
    )


def _block(row: list[Any]) -> list[Any]:
    """O:R строки: название, день, сумма, валюта."""
    cells = list(row[COLUMN_O : COLUMN_O + 4])
    return cells + [""] * (4 - len(cells))


def _forecast(
    rows: list[list[Any]],
) -> tuple[tuple[Event, ...] | None, Goal | None]:
    start = next(
        (
            index
            for index, row in enumerate(rows)
            if _text(_block(row)[0]).startswith(LIST_HEADER)
        ),
        None,
    )
    if start is None:
        return None, None
    events: list[Event] = []
    goal: Goal | None = None
    for index in range(start + 1, len(rows)):
        name, day, amount, currency = _block(rows[index])
        label = _text(name)
        if label == TABLE_HEADER or len(events) == MAX_EVENTS:
            break
        if GOAL_LABEL in label:
            code = _text(currency).upper() or "UZS"
            goal = Goal(index + 1, _number(amount) or 0.0, code)
            break
        number = _number(day)
        events.append(
            Event(
                slot=len(events),
                row=index + 1,
                name=label or None,
                day=None if number is None else int(number),
                amount=_number(amount),
                currency=_text(currency).upper() or None,
            )
        )
    return tuple(events), goal


def read_plan(rows: list[list[Any]]) -> MonthPlan:
    """Значения вкладки месяца (UNFORMATTED) → статьи, список и цель."""
    events, goal = _forecast(rows)
    return MonthPlan(_articles(rows), events, goal)
```

### Task 2: Подсказки по истории

**Files:**
- Modify: `app/services/plan.py`
- Test: `tests/test_month_plan.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `article_key` (Task 1); `transactions_frame` (`app/services/analytics_service.py`) — колонки `day` (date), `subcategory`, `currency`, `amount`.
- Produces: `shift_month(first: date, months: int) -> date`; `History(average_months: tuple[date, ...], last_month: date, current_month: date, current_days: int, sums: dict[str, tuple[float, float, float]])` с `.of(key) -> tuple[float, float, float]` (среднее, прошлый, текущий); `article_history(frame: pd.DataFrame, today: date) -> History`.

Дописать в `tests/test_month_plan.py` — импорты в начало файла:

```python
from datetime import date

import pytest

from app.services.analytics_service import transactions_frame
from app.services.plan import article_history, shift_month
from tests.test_day_budget import FACT_HEADER, RATES, fact_row
```

и тесты в конец:

```python
def facts(*rows: list[Any]) -> Any:
    return transactions_frame([FACT_HEADER, *rows], RATES)


def test_history_is_three_full_months_the_last_one_and_this_month() -> None:
    frame = facts(
        fact_row(date(2026, 6, 30), "🍔 ЕДА", "кофе", 5_000_000),  # до окна
        fact_row(date(2026, 7, 5), "🍔 ЕДА", "кофе", 300_000),
        fact_row(date(2026, 8, 5), "🍔 ЕДА", "кофе", 600_000),
        fact_row(date(2026, 9, 5), "🍔 ЕДА", "кофе", 900_000),
        fact_row(date(2026, 9, 6), "🍔 ЕДА", "кофе", -100_000),  # возврат
        fact_row(date(2026, 10, 2), "🍔 ЕДА", "кофе", 250_000),
        fact_row(date(2026, 10, 12), "🍔 ЕДА", "кофе", 7_000_000),  # после сегодня
    )
    history = article_history(frame, date(2026, 10, 11))
    assert history.average_months == (
        date(2026, 7, 1),
        date(2026, 8, 1),
        date(2026, 9, 1),
    )
    assert (history.last_month, history.current_month, history.current_days) == (
        date(2026, 9, 1),
        date(2026, 10, 1),
        11,
    )
    average, last, current = history.of("кофе|UZS")
    assert average == pytest.approx(1_700_000 / 3)
    assert (last, current) == (800_000, 250_000)


def test_months_without_records_are_zero_and_currencies_apart() -> None:
    frame = facts(
        fact_row(date(2026, 9, 5), "🍔 ЕДА", "кофе", 900_000),
        fact_row(date(2026, 9, 5), "🍔 ЕДА", "кофе", 30, "USD"),
    )
    history = article_history(frame, date(2026, 10, 11))
    assert history.of("кофе|UZS") == (300_000, 900_000, 0)
    assert history.of("кофе|USD") == (10, 30, 0)
    assert history.of("кафе|UZS") == (0, 0, 0)


def test_months_go_back_across_the_new_year() -> None:
    assert shift_month(date(2027, 1, 1), -3) == date(2026, 10, 1)
    assert shift_month(date(2026, 12, 1), 1) == date(2027, 1, 1)
    history = article_history(facts(), date(2027, 1, 3))
    assert history.average_months == (
        date(2026, 10, 1),
        date(2026, 11, 1),
        date(2026, 12, 1),
    )
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Подсказки**

В `app/services/plan.py` заменить блок импортов на:

```python
from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
from datetime import date
from typing import Any

import pandas as pd

from app.services.day_budget import COLUMN_O, GOAL_LABEL, LIST_HEADER, TABLE_HEADER
```

и дописать в конец:

```python
def shift_month(first: date, months: int) -> date:
    """Первое число месяца на months вперёд (назад — с минусом)."""
    index = first.year * 12 + first.month - 1 + months
    return date(index // 12, index % 12 + 1, 1)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class History:
    """Подсказки: среднее за три полных месяца, прошлый и текущий месяц."""

    average_months: tuple[date, ...]
    last_month: date
    current_month: date
    current_days: int
    sums: dict[str, tuple[float, float, float]]  # ключ → (среднее, прошлый, текущий)

    def of(self, key: str) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
        return self.sums.get(key, (0.0, 0.0, 0.0))


def article_history(frame: pd.DataFrame, today: date) -> History:
    """Суммы листа fact по паре (подкатегория, валюта) в валюте строки.

    Знак как во вкладке: трата и доход плюс, возврат минус. Месяцы считаются от
    сегодняшней даты, месяц без записей — ноль.
    """
    current = today.replace(day=1)
    months = tuple(shift_month(current, -back) for back in (3, 2, 1))
    window = frame[(frame["day"] >= months[0]) & (frame["day"] <= today)]
    by_key: dict[str, dict[date, float]] = {}
    if not window.empty:
        month = window["day"].map(lambda value: value.replace(day=1))
        totals = (
            window.assign(month=month)
            .groupby(["subcategory", "currency", "month"])["amount"]
            .sum()
        )
        for (sub, currency, first), amount in totals.items():
            by_key.setdefault(article_key(sub, currency), {})[first] = float(amount)
    sums = {
        key: (
            sum(values.get(first, 0.0) for first in months) / len(months),
            values.get(months[-1], 0.0),
            values.get(current, 0.0),
        )
        for key, values in by_key.items()
    }
    return History(months, months[-1], current, today.day, sums)
```

```python
EMPTY: EventCells = (None, None, None, None)

def test_changes_must_point_to_editable_rows_and_known_currencies() -> None:
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Правки**

В `app/services/plan.py` заменить блок импортов на:

```python
from collections.abc import Collection
from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
from datetime import date
from typing import Any

import pandas as pd

from app.domain import Rates
from app.services.day_budget import COLUMN_O, GOAL_LABEL, LIST_HEADER, TABLE_HEADER
```

и дописать в конец:

```python
TOLERANCE = 0.005  # суммы сравниваем до копейки


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EventValue:
    """Новое содержимое строки списка: сумма со знаком (+ поступление, − платёж)."""

    name: str
    day: int
    amount: float
    currency: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ArticleChange:
    key: str
    base: float  # план, который видел пользователь
    plan: float


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EventChange:
    slot: int
    base: EventCells  # строка списка, которую видел пользователь
    value: EventValue | None  # None — очистить строку


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class GoalChange:
    base: tuple[float, str]
    value: tuple[float, str]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PlanChanges:
    articles: tuple[ArticleChange, ...] = ()
    events: tuple[EventChange, ...] = ()
    goal: GoalChange | None = None

    def count(self) -> int:
        return len(self.articles) + len(self.events) + int(self.goal is not None)


def _same(left: float | None, right: float | None) -> bool:
    if left is None or right is None:
        return left is None and right is None
    return abs(left - right) < TOLERANCE


def unknown(
    plan: MonthPlan, changes: PlanChanges, currencies: Collection[str]
) -> set[str]:
    """Поля, которые во вкладке не найти или нельзя править (ответ 422)."""
    bad: set[str] = set()
    editable = {a.key for a in plan.articles if a.editable}
    keys = [change.key for change in changes.articles]
    if len(set(keys)) != len(keys) or any(key not in editable for key in keys):
        bad.add("rows")
    slots = {event.slot for event in plan.events or ()}
    chosen = [change.slot for change in changes.events]
    if len(set(chosen)) != len(chosen) or any(
        change.slot not in slots
        or (change.value is not None and change.value.currency not in currencies)
        for change in changes.events
    ):
        bad.add("events")
    if changes.goal is not None and (
        plan.goal is None or changes.goal.value[1] not in currencies
    ):
        bad.add("goal")
    return bad


def conflicts(plan: MonthPlan, changes: PlanChanges) -> list[str]:
    """Что поменялось во вкладке после открытия плана: ключи, «event:<n>», «goal»."""
    found: list[str] = []
    articles = {a.key: a for a in plan.articles}
    for change in changes.articles:
        if not _same(articles[change.key].plan, change.base):
            found.append(change.key)
    events = {e.slot: e for e in plan.events or ()}
    for change in changes.events:
        name, day, amount, currency = events[change.slot].cells()
        base_name, base_day, base_amount, base_currency = change.base
        if (name, day, currency) != (base_name, base_day, base_currency) or not _same(
            amount, base_amount
        ):
            found.append(f"event:{change.slot}")
    if changes.goal is not None and plan.goal is not None:
        amount, currency = changes.goal.base
        if currency != plan.goal.currency or not _same(plan.goal.amount, amount):
            found.append("goal")
    return found


def cell_updates(
    title: str, plan: MonthPlan, changes: PlanChanges
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Диапазоны для values_batch_update (RAW): D статей, O:R списка, Q:R цели."""
    articles = {a.key: a for a in plan.articles}
    events = {e.slot: e for e in plan.events or ()}
    data: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for change in changes.articles:
        row = articles[change.key].row
        data.append({"range": f"'{title}'!D{row}", "values": [[0.0 - change.plan]]})
    for change in changes.events:
        row = events[change.slot].row
        value = change.value
        cells: list[Any] = (
            ["", "", "", ""]
            if value is None
            else [value.name, value.day, value.amount, value.currency]
        )
        data.append({"range": f"'{title}'!O{row}:R{row}", "values": [cells]})
    if changes.goal is not None and plan.goal is not None:
        amount, currency = changes.goal.value
        row = plan.goal.row
        data.append({"range": f"'{title}'!Q{row}:R{row}", "values": [[amount, currency]]})
    return data


def _filled(event: Event, value: EventValue | None) -> Event:
    if value is None:
        return replace(event, name=None, day=None, amount=None, currency=None)
    return replace(
        event,
        name=value.name,
        day=value.day,
        amount=value.amount,
        currency=value.currency,
    )


def applied(plan: MonthPlan, changes: PlanChanges) -> MonthPlan:
    """План после правок — для «Свободно» в уведомлении."""
    plans = {change.key: change.plan for change in changes.articles}
    values = {change.slot: change.value for change in changes.events}
    articles = tuple(replace(a, plan=plans.get(a.key, a.plan)) for a in plan.articles)
    events: tuple[Event, ...] | None = None
    if plan.events is not None:
        events = tuple(
            _filled(e, values[e.slot]) if e.slot in values else e for e in plan.events
        )
    goal = plan.goal
    if changes.goal is not None and goal is not None:
        amount, currency = changes.goal.value
        goal = replace(goal, amount=amount, currency=currency)
    return MonthPlan(articles, events, goal)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Totals:
    """Итог плана в сумах по курсам system (как колонки H–J вкладки)."""

    incomes: float  # строки списка со знаком «+»
    spend: float  # план статей «Расходы»
    goal: float
    income_rows: float  # план статей «Доходы» — для сравнения со списком

    @property
    def free(self) -> float:
        return self.incomes - self.spend - self.goal


def totals(plan: MonthPlan, rates: Rates) -> Totals:
    def uzs(amount: float, currency: str | None) -> float:
        return amount * rates.to_uzs.get(currency or "", 0.0)

    spend = sum(uzs(a.plan, a.currency) for a in plan.articles if a.kind == "spend")
    income_rows = sum(
        uzs(a.plan, a.currency) for a in plan.articles if a.kind == "income"
    )
    incomes = sum(
        uzs(e.amount, e.currency)
        for e in plan.events or ()
        if e.name and e.amount is not None and e.amount > 0
    )
    goal = uzs(plan.goal.amount, plan.goal.currency) if plan.goal else 0.0
    return Totals(incomes, spend, goal, income_rows)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Текст сообщения**

В `app/services/plan.py` заменить блок импортов на:

```python
from collections.abc import Collection
from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
from datetime import date
from typing import Any

import pandas as pd

from app.domain import Rates
from app.services.analytics_service import MONTHS_RU, money
from app.services.day_budget import (
    COLUMN_O,
    GOAL_LABEL,
    LIST_HEADER,
    TABLE_HEADER,
    short,
)
```

В конец файла:

```python
NOTE_LINES = 10  # дальше — «… и ещё N»


def _plain(value: float) -> str:
    """«1 500 000», «650,50»: копейки, только если они есть."""
    if float(value).is_integer():
        return money(value)
    return f"{value:,.2f}".replace(",", " ").replace(".", ",")


def _amount(value: float, currency: str) -> str:
    return _plain(value) if currency == "UZS" else f"{_plain(value)} {currency}"


def _event_text(cells: EventCells) -> str:
    name, day, amount, currency = cells
    if not name or day is None or amount is None or not currency:
        return "—"
    sign = "+" if amount > 0 else "−"
    return f"{day} · {name} {sign}{_amount(abs(amount), currency)}"


def change_note(
    plan: MonthPlan,
    changes: PlanChanges,
    rates: Rates,
    month: date,
    author: str | None,
    icons: dict[str, str],
) -> str:
    """Сообщение второму: кто менял, что было → стало и «Свободно» до и после."""
    articles = {a.key: a for a in plan.articles}
    lines: list[str] = []
    for change in changes.articles:
        article = articles[change.key]
        parts = (icons.get(article.subcategory, ""), article.subcategory)
        label = " ".join(part for part in parts if part)
        if article.currency != "UZS":
            label += f" {article.currency}"
        lines.append(f"{label}: {_plain(change.base)} → {_plain(change.plan)}")
    for change in changes.events:
        value = change.value
        after = (
            "удалено"
            if value is None
            else _event_text((value.name, value.day, value.amount, value.currency))
        )
        lines.append(f"📅 {_event_text(change.base)} → {after}")
    if changes.goal is not None:
        (was, was_code), (now, now_code) = changes.goal.base, changes.goal.value
        lines.append(f"🎯 Отложить: {_amount(was, was_code)} → {_amount(now, now_code)}")
    shown = lines[:NOTE_LINES]
    if len(lines) > NOTE_LINES:
        shown.append(f"… и ещё {len(lines) - NOTE_LINES}")
    name = MONTHS_RU[month.month - 1]
    head = (
        f"✏️ {author} — правки в плане на {name}:"
        if author
        else f"✏️ Правки в плане на {name}:"
    )
    before = totals(plan, rates).free
    after_free = totals(applied(plan, changes), rates).free
    return "\n".join([head, *shown, f"Свободно: {short(before)} → {short(after_free)}"])
```

- Produces: `GoogleSheetsService.write_cells(data: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> None`; `LoadedPlan(plan: MonthPlan, rates: Rates, history: History)`; `load_plan(gs: GoogleSheetsService, title: str, today: date) -> LoadedPlan | None`; `SaveOutcome(plan: MonthPlan, rates: Rates, invalid: frozenset[str] = frozenset(), conflicts: tuple[str, ...] = ())`; `save_plan(gs: GoogleSheetsService, title: str, changes: PlanChanges) -> SaveOutcome` (WorksheetNotFound пробрасывает). - [ ] **Step 1: Тесты**

В импорты `tests/test_month_plan.py` добавить:

```python
import re
import time
from types import SimpleNamespace
from unittest.mock import Mock

from gspread.exceptions import WorksheetNotFound

from app.services.google_sheets import GoogleSheetsService
from app.services.plan import load_plan, save_plan
```

в конец файла:

```python
class PlanSheets:
    """Вкладки в памяти: get_values, get_rates; write_cells меняет те же строки."""

    def __init__(
        self, tabs: dict[str, list[list[Any]]], fact: list[list[Any]] | None = None
    ) -> None:
        self.tabs = {name: [list(row) for row in rows] for name, rows in tabs.items()}
        self.tabs["fact"] = fact or [FACT_HEADER]
        self.writes: list[list[dict[str, Any]]] = []
        self.delay = 0.0  # пауза чтения: в тесте гонки оба чтения идут раньше записи

    def get_values(self, name: str) -> list[list[Any]]:
        time.sleep(self.delay)
        if name not in self.tabs:
            raise WorksheetNotFound(name)
        return [list(row) for row in self.tabs[name]]

    def get_rates(self) -> Rates:
        return Rates({"UZS": 1.0, "USD": 12_000.0, "RUB": 150.0})

    def write_cells(self, data: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
        self.writes.append(data)
        for item in data:
            found = re.fullmatch(r"'(.+)'!([A-Z])(\d+)(?::[A-Z]\d+)?", item["range"])
            assert found, item["range"]
            title, column, row = found.group(1), found.group(2), int(found.group(3))
            cells = self.tabs[title][row - 1]
            for offset, value in enumerate(item["values"][0]):
                cells[ord(column) - ord("A") + offset] = value


def test_load_plan_reads_the_tab_rates_and_history() -> None:
    sheets = PlanSheets(
        {"Oct 26": TAB},
        [FACT_HEADER, fact_row(date(2026, 9, 5), "🍔 ЕДА", "кофе", 900_000)],
    )
    loaded = load_plan(sheets, "Oct 26", date(2026, 10, 11))
    assert loaded is not None
    assert [a.key for a in loaded.plan.articles] == [
        "кофе|UZS",
        "квартплата|USD",
        "зарплата|UZS",
    ]
    assert loaded.rates.to_uzs["USD"] == 12_000
    assert loaded.history.of("кофе|UZS") == (300_000, 900_000, 0)


def test_load_plan_without_the_tab_is_none() -> None:
    assert load_plan(PlanSheets({}), "Nov 26", date(2026, 10, 11)) is None


def test_save_plan_writes_and_the_tab_shows_the_new_plan() -> None:
    sheets = PlanSheets({"Oct 26": TAB})
    changes = PlanChanges(articles=(ArticleChange("кофе|UZS", 1_500_000, 1_800_000),))
    outcome = save_plan(sheets, "Oct 26", changes)
    assert (outcome.invalid, outcome.conflicts) == (frozenset(), ())
    assert outcome.plan.articles[0].plan == 1_500_000  # вкладка до записи
    assert read_plan(sheets.get_values("Oct 26")).articles[0].plan == 1_800_000


def test_a_row_inserted_above_by_hand_still_gets_the_write() -> None:
    sheets = PlanSheets({"Oct 26": TAB})
    tab = sheets.tabs["Oct 26"]
    tab.insert(1, ["Расходы", "🚌 ТРАНСПОРТ", "такси", -1, 0, 1, "UZS"] + [""] * 12)
    changes = PlanChanges(articles=(ArticleChange("кофе|UZS", 1_500_000, 1_800_000),))
    save_plan(sheets, "Oct 26", changes)
    assert sheets.writes[0][0]["range"] == "'Oct 26'!D3"  # кофе съехал на строку 3


def test_save_plan_with_a_conflict_writes_nothing() -> None:
    sheets = PlanSheets({"Oct 26": TAB})
    changes = PlanChanges(articles=(ArticleChange("кофе|UZS", 1_000_000, 1_800_000),))
    outcome = save_plan(sheets, "Oct 26", changes)
    assert outcome.conflicts == ("кофе|UZS",)
    assert sheets.writes == []


def test_save_plan_with_unknown_rows_writes_nothing() -> None:
    sheets = PlanSheets({"Oct 26": TAB})
    changes = PlanChanges(articles=(ArticleChange("кальян|UZS", 0, 1),))
    outcome = save_plan(sheets, "Oct 26", changes)
    assert outcome.invalid == frozenset({"rows"})
    assert sheets.writes == []


def test_write_cells_sends_one_raw_request() -> None:
    book = SimpleNamespace(values_batch_update=Mock())
    service = GoogleSheetsService.__new__(GoogleSheetsService)
    service.client, service.sheet = None, book
    service._worksheets, service._balance_cells = {}, None
    data = [{"range": "'Oct 26'!D2", "values": [[-1.0]]}]
    service.write_cells(data)
    service.write_cells([])
    book.values_batch_update.assert_called_once_with(
        {"valueInputOption": "RAW", "data": data}
    )
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Запись ячеек**

В `app/services/google_sheets.py` после метода `get_values`:

```python
    def write_cells(self, data: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
        """Значения как есть (RAW) одним запросом: применяются все или ни одно.

        Повтор после переподключения безопасен: те же значения в тех же ячейках.
        """
        if not data:
            return
        self._with_retry(
            "Writing cells",
            lambda: self.sheet.values_batch_update(
                {"valueInputOption": "RAW", "data": data}
            ),
        )
```

```python
from collections.abc import Collection

from typing import Any

import pandas as pd
from gspread.exceptions import WorksheetNotFound

from app import config
```

В конец файла:

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LoadedPlan:
    plan: MonthPlan
    rates: Rates
    history: History


def load_plan(gs: GoogleSheetsService, title: str, today: date) -> LoadedPlan | None:
    """Вкладка месяца, курсы и подсказки (синхронно); None — вкладки нет."""
    try:
        rows = gs.get_values(title)
    except WorksheetNotFound:
        return None
    rates = gs.get_rates()
    frame = transactions_frame(gs.get_values(config.FACT_SHEET_NAME), rates)
    return LoadedPlan(read_plan(rows), rates, article_history(frame, today))


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SaveOutcome:
    plan: MonthPlan  # вкладка до записи
    rates: Rates
    invalid: frozenset[str] = frozenset()
    conflicts: tuple[str, ...] = ()


def save_plan(
    gs: GoogleSheetsService, title: str, changes: PlanChanges
) -> SaveOutcome:
    """Проверить правки по свежей вкладке и записать; при ошибке ничего не пишет.

    WorksheetNotFound — вкладки месяца нет.
    """
    plan = read_plan(gs.get_values(title))
    rates = gs.get_rates()
    if bad := unknown(plan, changes, rates.to_uzs.keys()):
        return SaveOutcome(plan, rates, invalid=frozenset(bad))
    if found := conflicts(plan, changes):
        return SaveOutcome(plan, rates, conflicts=tuple(found))
    gs.write_cells(cell_updates(title, plan, changes))
    return SaveOutcome(plan, rates)
```

```python
"""API вкладки «План»: план месяца, сохранение, конфликты, уведомления."""

from datetime import timedelta
from typing import Any

import pytest

from app.domain import month_title
from app.services.plan import shift_month
from tests.test_day_budget import FACT_HEADER, fact_row
from tests.test_month_plan import TAB, PlanSheets
from tests.test_web_api import OWNER, TODAY, auth, client_for, make_application

CURRENT = TODAY.replace(day=1)
NEXT = shift_month(CURRENT, 1)
MONTH = f"{CURRENT:%Y-%m}"
TITLE = month_title(CURRENT)


def sheets_with() -> PlanSheets:
    previous = shift_month(CURRENT, -1) + timedelta(days=4)
    fact = [FACT_HEADER, fact_row(previous, "🍔 ЕДА", "кофе", 900_000)]
    return PlanSheets({TITLE: TAB}, fact)


def get_plan(sheets: Any, month: str = MONTH, user: int = OWNER) -> Any:
    application = make_application(sheets)
    return client_for(application).get(
        f"/api/plan?month={month}", headers=auth(user)
    )


def test_plan_has_rows_with_hints_events_goal_and_rates() -> None:
    body = get_plan(sheets_with()).json()
    assert (body["status"], body["month"], body["title"]) == ("ok", MONTH, TITLE)
    assert body["rows"][0] == {
        "key": "кофе|UZS",
        "kind": "spend",
        "group": "🍔 ЕДА",
        "subcategory": "кофе",
        "icon": "☕",
        "currency": "UZS",
        "plan": 1_500_000,
        "average": 300_000,

        "current": 0,

        "slot": 0,
        "name": "Аванс",
        "day": 15,
        "amount": 3_000_000,
        "currency": "UZS",

    assert body["events"][2]["name"] is None
    assert body["goal"] == {"amount": 1_000_000, "currency": "UZS"}
    assert body["rates"]["USD"] == 12_000
    assert body["history"]["last"] == f"{shift_month(CURRENT, -1):%Y-%m}"

@pytest.mark.parametrize("month", ["", "2026-13", "1999-01", "abc"])
def test_other_months_are_refused(month: str) -> None:

    response = get_plan(Broken({}))

        "sheets_unavailable",
    )


def test_plan_needs_a_signature() -> None:
    client = client_for(make_application(sheets_with()))
    assert client.get(f"/api/plan?month={MONTH}").status_code == 401
```

В конец `app/web/schemas.py`:

```python
class PlanHistoryOut(ApiModel):
    average: list[str]  # три полных месяца «YYYY-MM»
    last: str
    current: str
    current_days: int


class PlanArticleOut(ApiModel):
    key: str
    kind: Literal["spend", "income"]
    group: str
    subcategory: str
    icon: str
    currency: str
    plan: float  # положительный, в валюте строки
    average: float
    last: float
    current: float
    editable: bool


class PlanEventOut(ApiModel):
    slot: int
    name: str | None
    day: int | None
    amount: float | None  # со знаком: «+» поступление, «−» платёж
    currency: str | None


class PlanGoalOut(ApiModel):
    amount: float
    currency: str


class PlanOut(ApiModel):
    """План месяца для экрана «План»; без вкладки — только статус."""

    month: str
    title: str
    status: Literal["ok", "not_created", "no_month_tab"]
    today: date
    rates: dict[str, float] = {}
    history: PlanHistoryOut | None = None
    rows: list[PlanArticleOut] = []
    events: list[PlanEventOut] | None = None  # None — во вкладке нет блока прогноза
    goal: PlanGoalOut | None = None
    saved: int | None = None  # после сохранения — сколько правок записано
```

В `app/web/api.py`:


и в список из `app.web.schemas` — `PlanArticleOut`, `PlanEventOut`, `PlanGoalOut`, `PlanHistoryOut`, `PlanOut`.

2. В `FIELD_MESSAGES` добавить:

```python

```

3. Перед `def routes(`:

```python
def plan_month(text: str | None, today: date) -> date | None:
    """«2026-10» → 01.10.2026, если это текущий или следующий месяц."""
    try:
        first = datetime.strptime(text or "", "%Y-%m").date()
    except ValueError:
        return None
    current = today.replace(day=1)
    return first if first in (current, shift_month(current, 1)) else None


def plan_out(
    first: date, today: date, loaded: LoadedPlan | None, icons: dict[str, str]
) -> PlanOut:
    """План месяца для экрана; без вкладки — только статус."""
    month, title = f"{first:%Y-%m}", month_title(first)
    if loaded is None:
        status = "no_month_tab" if first == today.replace(day=1) else "not_created"
        return PlanOut(month=month, title=title, status=status, today=today)
    history, plan = loaded.history, loaded.plan
    rows = []
    for article in plan.articles:
        average, last, current = history.of(article.key)
        rows.append(
            PlanArticleOut(
                key=article.key,
                kind=article.kind,
                group=article.group,
                subcategory=article.subcategory,
                icon=icons.get(article.subcategory, ""),
                currency=article.currency,
                plan=article.plan,
                average=average,
                last=last,
                current=current,
                editable=article.editable,
            )
        )
    events = None
    if plan.events is not None:
        events = [
            PlanEventOut(
                slot=e.slot, name=e.name, day=e.day, amount=e.amount, currency=e.currency
            )
            for e in plan.events
        ]
    goal = None
    if plan.goal is not None:
        goal = PlanGoalOut(amount=plan.goal.amount, currency=plan.goal.currency)
    return PlanOut(
        month=month,
        title=title,
        status="ok",
        today=today,
        rates=dict(loaded.rates.to_uzs),
        history=PlanHistoryOut(
            average=[f"{m:%Y-%m}" for m in history.average_months],
            last=f"{history.last_month:%Y-%m}",
            current=f"{history.current_month:%Y-%m}",
            current_days=history.current_days,
        ),
        rows=rows,
        events=events,
        goal=goal,
    )
```

```python
    async def month_plan(request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
        user = current_user(request)
        today = local_today(config.ANALYTICS_TIMEZONE)
        first = plan_month(request.query_params.get("month"), today)
        if first is None:
            return validation_error({"month"})
        gs = application.bot_data["gs_service"]
        try:
            loaded = await asyncio.to_thread(load_plan, gs, month_title(first), today)
        except Exception:
            logger.exception("Plan failed for user_id=%s", user.id)
            return error_response(503, "sheets_unavailable", SHEETS_DOWN)
        icons: dict[str, str] = application.bot_data.get("icons", {})
        body = plan_out(first, today, loaded, icons)
        return JSONResponse(body.model_dump(mode="json"))
```

и в список маршрутов — `Route("/plan", month_plan),`.

```python
EMPTY_CELLS = {"name": None, "day": None, "amount": None, "currency": None}


def change(**over: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    body: dict[str, Any] = {
        "month": MONTH,
        "rows": [{"key": "кофе|UZS", "base": 1_500_000, "plan": "1800000"}],
        "events": [],
        "goal": None,
    }
    return {**body, **over}


def plan_app(sheets: PlanSheets | None = None) -> Any:
    application = make_application(sheets or sheets_with())
    application.bot.get_chat = AsyncMock(return_value=SimpleNamespace(first_name="Иван"))
    return application


def post_plan(application: Any, body: dict[str, Any], user: int = OWNER) -> Any:
    return client_for(application).post("/api/plan", json=body, headers=auth(user))


def test_save_writes_the_cells_and_answers_with_the_fresh_plan() -> None:
    sheets = sheets_with()
    response = post_plan(plan_app(sheets), change())
    assert response.status_code == 200
    body = response.json()
    assert (body["saved"], body["rows"][0]["plan"]) == (1, 1_800_000)
    assert sheets.writes == [[{"range": f"'{TITLE}'!D2", "values": [[-1_800_000.0]]}]]


def test_cents_of_a_currency_row_reach_the_sheet() -> None:
    sheets = sheets_with()
    rows = [{"key": "квартплата|USD", "base": 500, "plan": "650.5"}]
    assert post_plan(plan_app(sheets), change(rows=rows)).status_code == 200
    assert sheets.writes[0][0]["values"] == [[-650.5]]


def test_events_and_goal_are_saved() -> None:
    sheets = sheets_with()
    body = change(
        rows=[],
        events=[
            {
                "slot": 2,
                "base": EMPTY_CELLS,
                "value": {
                    "name": "Премия",
                    "day": 25,
                    "amount": "1000000",
                    "currency": "UZS",
                },
            },
            {
                "slot": 1,
                "base": {
                    "name": "Квартплата",
                    "day": 18,
                    "amount": -500,
                    "currency": "USD",
                },
                "value": {
                    "name": "Квартплата",
                    "day": 18,
                    "amount": "-650.5",
                    "currency": "USD",
                },
            },
        ],
        goal={
            "base": {"amount": 1_000_000, "currency": "UZS"},
            "value": {"amount": "2000000", "currency": "UZS"},
        },
    )
    response = post_plan(plan_app(sheets), body)
    assert response.status_code == 200
    fresh = response.json()
    assert fresh["events"][2] == {
        "slot": 2,
        "name": "Премия",
        "day": 25,
        "amount": 1_000_000,
        "currency": "UZS",
    }
    assert fresh["events"][1]["amount"] == -650.5
    assert fresh["goal"] == {"amount": 2_000_000, "currency": "UZS"}
    assert fresh["saved"] == 3


def test_the_other_user_is_told_and_the_author_is_not() -> None:
    application = plan_app()

def test_a_failed_note_does_not_fail_the_save() -> None:
    application = plan_app()
    application.bot.send_message = AsyncMock(side_effect=RuntimeError("blocked"))
    assert post_plan(application, change()).status_code == 200


def test_a_conflict_writes_nothing_and_returns_the_fresh_plan() -> None:
    sheets = sheets_with()
    application = plan_app(sheets)
    rows = [{"key": "кофе|UZS", "base": 1_000_000, "plan": "1800000"}]
    response = post_plan(application, change(rows=rows))
    assert response.status_code == 409

    assert body["plan"]["rows"][0]["plan"] == 1_500_000
    assert sheets.writes == []
    application.bot.send_message.assert_not_awaited()

            "events",
        ),
        (
            change(
                rows=[],
                goal={
                    "base": {"amount": 1_000_000, "currency": "UZS"},
                    "value": {"amount": "5", "currency": "EUR"},
                },
            ),
            "goal",
        ),
        (change(rows=[]), "changes"),
        (change(month="1999-01"), "month"),
    ],
)
def test_bad_saves_name_the_field(body: dict[str, Any], field: str) -> None:
    sheets = sheets_with()
    response = post_plan(plan_app(sheets), body)
    assert response.status_code == 422
    assert list(response.json()["error"]["fields"]) == [field]
    assert sheets.writes == []


def test_save_sheet_failure_is_503() -> None:
    class Broken(PlanSheets):
        def write_cells(self, data: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
            raise ConnectionError("Google is down")

    response = post_plan(plan_app(Broken({TITLE: TAB})), change())
    assert (response.status_code, response.json()["error"]["code"]) == (
        503,
        "sheets_unavailable",
    )


async def test_two_saves_at_once_cannot_both_win() -> None:
    sheets = sheets_with()
    sheets.delay = 0.05  # без замка оба прочитали бы вкладку до первой записи
    transport = httpx.ASGITransport(app=create_app(plan_app(sheets), "s"))
    other = [{"key": "кофе|UZS", "base": 1_500_000, "plan": "1700000"}]
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(transport=transport, base_url="http://test") as client:
        answers = await asyncio.gather(
            client.post("/api/plan", json=change(), headers=auth()),
            client.post("/api/plan", json=change(rows=other), headers=auth(WIFE)),
        )
    assert sorted(a.status_code for a in answers) == [200, 409]
    assert len(sheets.writes) == 1
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Схемы запроса**

В `app/web/schemas.py` заменить импорт pydantic на `from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator` и дописать в конец:

```python
MAX_SUM = Decimal(10) ** 12


class EventValueIn(ApiModel):
    """Строка списка: сумма со знаком — «+» поступление, «−» платёж."""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", str_strip_whitespace=True)

    name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=40)
    day: int = Field(ge=1, le=31)
    amount: Decimal = Field(ge=-MAX_SUM, le=MAX_SUM, decimal_places=2)
    currency: str = Field(pattern=r"^[A-Z]{3}$")

    @field_validator("amount")
    @classmethod
    def not_zero(cls, value: Decimal) -> Decimal:
        if value == 0:
            raise ValueError("сумма — не 0")
        return value


class EventCellsIn(ApiModel):
    """Строка списка, какой её видел пользователь (пустые поля — null)."""

    name: str | None
    day: int | None
    amount: float | None
    currency: str | None


class ArticleChangeIn(ApiModel):
    key: str = Field(min_length=3, max_length=200)
    base: float
    plan: Decimal = Field(ge=0, le=MAX_SUM, decimal_places=2)


class EventChangeIn(ApiModel):
    slot: int = Field(ge=0, le=50)
    base: EventCellsIn
    value: EventValueIn | None


class GoalIn(ApiModel):
    amount: Decimal = Field(ge=0, le=MAX_SUM, decimal_places=2)
    currency: str = Field(pattern=r"^[A-Z]{3}$")


class GoalBaseIn(ApiModel):
    amount: float
    currency: str


class GoalChangeIn(ApiModel):
    base: GoalBaseIn
    value: GoalIn


class PlanSaveIn(ApiModel):
    month: str = Field(pattern=r"^\d{4}-\d{2}$")
    rows: list[ArticleChangeIn] = Field(default=[], max_length=300)
    events: list[EventChangeIn] = Field(default=[], max_length=30)
    goal: GoalChangeIn | None = None


class PlanConflictOut(ApiModel):
    """409: план поменяли после открытия — свежий план и что именно поменялось."""

    error: ErrorOut
    plan: PlanOut
    conflicts: list[str]
```

```python
PLAN_LOCK = "plan_lock"  # bot_data: одно сохранение плана за раз — проверка и запись
```

4. Перед `def routes(`:

```python
def plan_changes(body: PlanSaveIn) -> PlanChanges:
    """Тело запроса → правки для services.plan (суммы — float, как в таблице)."""
    goal = None
    if body.goal is not None:
        goal = GoalChange(
            (body.goal.base.amount, body.goal.base.currency),
            (float(body.goal.value.amount), body.goal.value.currency),
        )
    return PlanChanges(
        articles=tuple(
            ArticleChange(c.key, c.base, float(c.plan)) for c in body.rows
        ),
        events=tuple(
            EventChange(
                c.slot,
                (c.base.name, c.base.day, c.base.amount, c.base.currency),
                None
                if c.value is None
                else EventValue(
                    c.value.name, c.value.day, float(c.value.amount), c.value.currency
                ),
            )
            for c in body.events
        ),
        goal=goal,
    )


async def author_name(bot: Bot, user: WebUser) -> str | None:
    """Имя для уведомления: из initData, иначе из чата с ботом."""
    if user.first_name:
        return user.first_name
    try:
        chat = await bot.get_chat(user.id)
    except Exception:
        logger.warning("No name for user_id=%s", user.id)
        return None
    return chat.first_name or None


async def notify_others(bot: Bot, author_id: int, text: str) -> None:
    """Сообщение всем пользователям, кроме автора; сбой отправки — только в лог."""
    for user_id in sorted(config.ALLOWED_USER_IDS - {author_id}):
        try:
            await bot.send_message(chat_id=user_id, text=text)
        except Exception:
            logger.warning("Could not tell user_id=%s about the plan", user_id)
```

5. Внутри `routes()` после `month_plan`:

```python
    async def save_month_plan(request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
        user = current_user(request)
        try:
            body = PlanSaveIn.model_validate_json(await request.body())
        except ValidationError as error:
            return validation_error(
                {str(e["loc"][0]) if e["loc"] else "body" for e in error.errors()}
            )
        today = local_today(config.ANALYTICS_TIMEZONE)
        first = plan_month(body.month, today)
        if first is None:
            return validation_error({"month"})
        changes = plan_changes(body)
        if changes.count() == 0:
            return validation_error({"changes"})
        title = month_title(first)
        gs = application.bot_data["gs_service"]
        lock: asyncio.Lock = application.bot_data.setdefault(PLAN_LOCK, asyncio.Lock())
        try:
            async with lock:
                outcome = await asyncio.to_thread(save_plan, gs, title, changes)
                loaded = None
                if not outcome.invalid:
                    loaded = await asyncio.to_thread(load_plan, gs, title, today)
        except WorksheetNotFound:
            return validation_error({"month"})
        except Exception:
            logger.exception("Plan save failed for user_id=%s", user.id)
            return error_response(503, "sheets_unavailable", SHEETS_DOWN)
        if outcome.invalid:
            return validation_error(set(outcome.invalid))
        icons: dict[str, str] = application.bot_data.get("icons", {})
        fresh = plan_out(first, today, loaded, icons)
        if outcome.conflicts:
            conflict = PlanConflictOut(
                error=ErrorOut(code="plan_changed", message=CONFLICT),
                plan=fresh,
                conflicts=list(outcome.conflicts),
            )
            return JSONResponse(conflict.model_dump(mode="json"), status_code=409)
        fresh.saved = changes.count()
        logger.info(
            "Plan %s: %s changes by user_id=%s", title, changes.count(), user.id
        )
        name = await author_name(application.bot, user)
        note = change_note(outcome.plan, changes, outcome.rates, first, name, icons)
        await notify_others(application.bot, user.id, note)
        return JSONResponse(fresh.model_dump(mode="json"))
```

и в список маршрутов после `Route("/plan", month_plan),` — `Route("/plan", save_month_plan, methods=["POST"]),`.

В `webapp/src/api.ts` после интерфейса `Dashboard`:

```ts
export interface PlanArticle {
  key: string;
  kind: "spend" | "income";
  group: string;
  subcategory: string;
  icon: string;
  currency: string;
  plan: number;
  average: number;
  last: number;
  current: number;
  editable: boolean;
}

export interface PlanEvent {
  slot: number;
  name: string | null;
  day: number | null;
  amount: number | null; // со знаком: «+» поступление, «−» платёж
  currency: string | null;
}

export interface PlanGoal {
  amount: number;
  currency: string;
}

export interface PlanHistory {
  average: string[];
  last: string;
  current: string;
  current_days: number;
}

export interface MonthPlan {
  month: string;
  title: string;
  status: "ok" | "not_created" | "no_month_tab";
  today: string;
  rates: Record<string, number>;
  history: PlanHistory | null;
  rows: PlanArticle[];
  events: PlanEvent[] | null;
  goal: PlanGoal | null;
  saved: number | null;
}

export interface EventValue {
  name: string;
  day: number;
  amount: string; // «1000000», «-650.5»
  currency: string;
}

export interface PlanSave {
  month: string;
  rows: { key: string; base: number; plan: string }[];
  events: {
    slot: number;
    base: Omit<PlanEvent, "slot">;
    value: EventValue | null;
  }[];
  goal: {
    base: PlanGoal;
    value: { amount: string; currency: string };
  } | null;
}

export interface PlanConflict {
  plan: MonthPlan;
  conflicts: string[];
}
```

В классе `ApiError` добавить поле и аргумент:

```ts
export class ApiError extends Error {
  readonly status: number;
  readonly code: string;
  readonly fields: Record<string, string>;
  readonly body: unknown; // всё тело ответа — у 409 там свежий план

  constructor(
    status: number,
    code: string,
    message: string,
    fields: Record<string, string> = {},
    body: unknown = null,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.status = status;
    this.code = code;
    this.fields = fields;
    this.body = body;
  }
}
```

В `request()` заменить `throw new ApiError(response.status, code, message, fields ?? {});` на:

```ts
      throw new ApiError(response.status, code, message, fields ?? {}, body);
```

После `request()`:

```ts
/** 409 сохранения плана: свежий план и что поменялось после открытия. */
export function planConflict(error: unknown): PlanConflict | null {
  if (!(error instanceof ApiError) || error.code !== "plan_changed") return null;
  const body = error.body;
  if (
    typeof body !== "object" ||
    body === null ||
    !("plan" in body) ||
    !("conflicts" in body)
  ) {
    return null;
  }
  return {
    plan: body.plan as MonthPlan,
    conflicts: body.conflicts as string[],
  };
}
```

В объект `api`:

```ts
  plan: (month: string) =>
    request<MonthPlan>(`/plan?month=${encodeURIComponent(month)}`),
  savePlan: (input: PlanSave) =>
    request<MonthPlan>("/plan", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify(input),
    }),
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  capital,
  days,
  monthDative,
  monthName,
  monthRange,
  monthShort,
  plural,
} from "./months";

describe("month names", () => {
  it("names months the Russian way", () => {
    expect(monthName("2026-09")).toBe("сентябрь");
    expect(monthShort("2026-09")).toBe("сен");
    expect(monthDative("2026-09")).toBe("сентябрю");
    expect(capital("октябрь")).toBe("Октябрь");
    expect(monthRange(["2026-07", "2026-08", "2026-09"])).toBe(
      "июль–сентябрь",
    );
  });

  it("puts numbers in the right form", () => {
    expect([1, 2, 5, 11, 21, 22, 112].map((n) => plural(n, "a", "b", "c"))).toEqual(
      ["a", "b", "c", "c", "a", "b", "c"],
    );
    expect(days(10)).toBe("10 дней");
    expect(days(1)).toBe("1 день");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Тесты черновика**

`webapp/src/plan/draft.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { PlanArticle, PlanEvent, PlanGoal } from "../api";
import { planFixture } from "../mocks/fixtures";
import {
  amountText,
  changeCount,
  editEvent,
  editGoal,
  editRow,
  emptyDraft,
  loadDraft,
  planTotals,
  rebase,
  storeDraft,
  toSave,
} from "./draft";

function row(key: string): PlanArticle {
  const found = planFixture.rows.find((item) => item.key === key);
  if (!found) throw new Error(key);
  return found;
}

function slot(number: number): PlanEvent {
  const found = (planFixture.events ?? []).find((item) => item.slot === number);
  if (!found) throw new Error(String(number));
  return found;
}

function goal(): PlanGoal {
  if (!planFixture.goal) throw new Error("goal");
  return planFixture.goal;
}

function withCoffee(plan: number) {
  return {
    ...planFixture,
    rows: planFixture.rows.map((item) =>
      item.key === "кофе|UZS" ? { ...item, plan } : item,
    ),
  };
}

describe("plan draft", () => {
  afterEach(() => {
    localStorage.clear();
  });

  it("keeps only real changes", () => {
    let draft = editRow(emptyDraft("2026-10"), row("кофе|UZS"), "1800000");
    expect(draft.rows).toEqual({
      "кофе|UZS": { base: 1_500_000, text: "1800000" },
    });
    draft = editRow(draft, row("кофе|UZS"), "1500000");
    expect(changeCount(draft)).toBe(0);
  });

  it("counts the month like the server", () => {
    expect(planTotals(planFixture, emptyDraft("2026-10"))).toEqual({
      incomes: 22_000_000,
      spend: 10_500_000,
      goal: 2_000_000,
      incomeRows: 20_000_000,
      free: 9_500_000,
    });
    const draft = editRow(emptyDraft("2026-10"), row("кофе|UZS"), "1800000");
    expect(planTotals(planFixture, draft).free).toBe(9_200_000);
  });

  it("adds, changes and clears rows of the list", () => {
    let draft = editEvent(emptyDraft("2026-10"), slot(3), {
      name: "Премия",
      day: 25,
      income: true,
      text: "1000000",
      currency: "UZS",
    });
    draft = editEvent(draft, slot(2), null);
    expect(planTotals(planFixture, draft).incomes).toBe(23_000_000);
    expect(toSave(draft).events).toEqual([
      {
        slot: 2,
        base: { name: "Квартплата", day: 18, amount: -500, currency: "USD" },
        value: null,
      },
      {
        slot: 3,
        base: { name: null, day: null, amount: null, currency: null },
        value: { name: "Премия", day: 25, amount: "1000000", currency: "UZS" },
      },
    ]);
  });

  it("sends payments with a minus and cents with a dot", () => {
    let draft = editRow(emptyDraft("2026-10"), row("квартплата|USD"), "650,5");
    draft = editEvent(draft, slot(2), {
      name: "Квартплата",
      day: 18,
      income: false,
      text: "650,5",
      currency: "USD",
    });
    const body = toSave(draft);
    expect(body.rows).toEqual([
      { key: "квартплата|USD", base: 500, plan: "650.5" },
    ]);
    expect(body.events[0]?.value?.amount).toBe("-650.5");
    expect(body.goal).toBeNull();
  });

  it("changes the goal and drops it when it is back", () => {
    let draft = editGoal(emptyDraft("2026-10"), goal(), "3000000", "UZS");
    expect(toSave(draft).goal).toEqual({
      base: { amount: 2_000_000, currency: "UZS" },
      value: { amount: "3000000", currency: "UZS" },
    });
    draft = editGoal(draft, goal(), "2000000", "UZS");
    expect(draft.goal).toBeNull();
  });

  it("rebases the draft on a fresh plan and names the conflicts", () => {
    const draft = editGoal(
      editRow(emptyDraft("2026-10"), row("кофе|UZS"), "1800000"),
      goal(),
      "3000000",
      "UZS",
    );
    const next = rebase(draft, withCoffee(1_600_000));
    expect(next.conflicts).toEqual(["кофе|UZS"]);
    expect(next.draft.rows["кофе|UZS"]).toEqual({
      base: 1_600_000,
      text: "1800000",
    });
    expect(next.draft.goal?.text).toBe("3000000");
  });

  it("drops an edit that the fresh plan already has", () => {
    const draft = editRow(emptyDraft("2026-10"), row("кофе|UZS"), "1800000");
    expect(changeCount(rebase(draft, withCoffee(1_800_000)).draft)).toBe(0);
  });

  it("keeps the draft in the phone between launches", () => {
    const draft = editRow(emptyDraft("2026-10"), row("кофе|UZS"), "1800000");
    storeDraft(1, draft);
    expect(loadDraft(1, "2026-10")).toEqual(draft);
    expect(loadDraft(2, "2026-10")).toBeNull();
    expect(loadDraft(1, "2026-11")).toBeNull();
    storeDraft(1, emptyDraft("2026-10"));
    expect(loadDraft(1, "2026-10")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("ignores a broken stored draft", () => {
    localStorage.setItem("plan-draft:1:2026-10", "{not json");
    expect(loadDraft(1, "2026-10")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("turns numbers into keypad text", () => {
    expect(amountText(1_234_567, false)).toBe("1234567");
    expect(amountText(12.5, true)).toBe("12,5");
    expect(amountText(500, true)).toBe("500");
    expect(amountText(0, false)).toBe("");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Месяцы**

`webapp/src/plan/months.ts`:

```ts
/** Названия месяцев и числа для подсказок плана: «сен», «сентябрю», «11 дней». */

const NOMINATIVE = [
  "январь",
  "февраль",
  "март",
  "апрель",
  "май",
  "июнь",
  "июль",
  "август",
  "сентябрь",
  "октябрь",
  "ноябрь",
  "декабрь",
];
const DATIVE = [
  "январю",
  "февралю",
  "марту",
  "апрелю",
  "маю",
  "июню",
  "июлю",
  "августу",
  "сентябрю",
  "октябрю",
  "ноябрю",
  "декабрю",
];
const SHORT = [
  "янв",
  "фев",
  "мар",
  "апр",
  "май",
  "июн",
  "июл",
  "авг",
  "сен",
  "окт",
  "ноя",
  "дек",
];

/** «2026-09» → 8. */
function index(month: string): number {
  return Number(month.slice(5, 7)) - 1;
}

export function monthName(month: string): string {
  return NOMINATIVE[index(month)] ?? month;
}

export function monthShort(month: string): string {
  return SHORT[index(month)] ?? month;
}

export function monthDative(month: string): string {
  return DATIVE[index(month)] ?? month;
}

export function capital(text: string): string {
  return text.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + text.slice(1);
}

/** «июль–сентябрь». */
export function monthRange(months: string[]): string {
  const first = months[0];
  const last = months[months.length - 1];
  return first && last ? `${monthName(first)}–${monthName(last)}` : "";
}

/** Русское число: 1 день, 2 дня, 5 дней, 11 дней, 21 день. */
export function plural(
  count: number,
  one: string,
  few: string,
  many: string,
): string {
  const tens = count % 100;
  const units = count % 10;
  if (tens >= 11 && tens <= 14) return many;
  if (units === 1) return one;
  if (units >= 2 && units <= 4) return few;
  return many;
}

export function days(count: number): string {
  return `${count} ${plural(count, "день", "дня", "дней")}`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Черновик**

`webapp/src/plan/draft.ts`:

```ts
/** Черновик плана: правки поверх плана из таблицы, итог, запрос на сохранение. */

import type {
  EventValue,
  MonthPlan,
  PlanArticle,
  PlanEvent,
  PlanGoal,
  PlanSave,
} from "../api";
import { amountValue, apiAmount } from "../entry/amount";

const EPSILON = 0.005; // суммы сравниваем до копейки, как сервер
const STORAGE = "plan-draft";

/** Правка статьи: base — план, который видели при открытии; text — как на клавиатуре. */
export interface RowEdit {
  base: number;
  text: string;
}

/** Новое содержимое строки списка: сумма без знака, знак — «Поступление» или «Платёж». */
export interface EventDraft {
  name: string;
  day: number;
  income: boolean;
  text: string;
  currency: string;
}

export interface EventEdit {
  base: PlanEvent;
  value: EventDraft | null; // null — очистить строку
}

export interface GoalEdit {
  base: PlanGoal;
  text: string;
  currency: string;
}

export interface Draft {
  month: string;
  rows: Record<string, RowEdit>;
  events: Record<string, EventEdit>; // ключ — номер строки списка
  goal: GoalEdit | null;
}

export interface PlanTotals {
  incomes: number;
  spend: number;
  goal: number;
  incomeRows: number;
  free: number;
}

export function emptyDraft(month: string): Draft {
  return { month, rows: {}, events: {}, goal: null };
}

export function changeCount(draft: Draft): number {
  return (
    Object.keys(draft.rows).length +
    Object.keys(draft.events).length +
    (draft.goal ? 1 : 0)
  );
}

function close(left: number | null, right: number | null): boolean {
  if (left === null || right === null) return left === right;
  return Math.abs(left - right) < EPSILON;
}

function without<T>(record: Record<string, T>, key: string): Record<string, T> {
  return Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(record).filter(([name]) => name !== key),
  );
}

export function eventAmount(value: EventDraft): number {
  return (value.income ? 1 : -1) * amountValue(value.text);
}

function sameEvent(event: PlanEvent, value: EventDraft | null): boolean {
  if (value === null) return event.name === null;
  return (
    event.name === value.name &&
    event.day === value.day &&
    event.currency === value.currency &&
    close(event.amount, eventAmount(value))
  );
}

function sameCells(left: PlanEvent, right: PlanEvent): boolean {
  return (
    left.name === right.name &&
    left.day === right.day &&
    left.currency === right.currency &&
    close(left.amount, right.amount)
  );
}

/** Сумма статьи в черновике; правка, равная плану, убирается. */
export function editRow(draft: Draft, article: PlanArticle, text: string): Draft {
  const base = draft.rows[article.key]?.base ?? article.plan;
  const rows = close(amountValue(text), base)
    ? without(draft.rows, article.key)
    : { ...draft.rows, [article.key]: { base, text } };
  return { ...draft, rows };
}

/** Строка списка в черновике; event — строка из плана, а не из черновика. */
export function editEvent(
  draft: Draft,
  event: PlanEvent,
  value: EventDraft | null,
): Draft {
  const key = String(event.slot);
  const base = draft.events[key]?.base ?? event;
  const events = sameEvent(base, value)
    ? without(draft.events, key)
    : { ...draft.events, [key]: { base, value } };
  return { ...draft, events };
}

export function editGoal(
  draft: Draft,
  goal: PlanGoal,
  text: string,
  currency: string,
): Draft {
  const base = draft.goal?.base ?? goal;
  const same =
    currency === base.currency && close(amountValue(text), base.amount);
  return { ...draft, goal: same ? null : { base, text, currency } };
}

export function rowPlan(draft: Draft, article: PlanArticle): number {
  const edit = draft.rows[article.key];
  return edit ? amountValue(edit.text) : article.plan;
}

export function shownEvents(plan: MonthPlan, draft: Draft): PlanEvent[] {
  return (plan.events ?? []).map((event) => {
    const edit = draft.events[String(event.slot)];
    if (!edit) return event;
    if (edit.value === null) {
      return {
        slot: event.slot,
        name: null,
        day: null,
        amount: null,
        currency: null,
      };
    }
    return {
      slot: event.slot,
      name: edit.value.name,
      day: edit.value.day,
      amount: eventAmount(edit.value),
      currency: edit.value.currency,
    };
  });
}

export function shownGoal(plan: MonthPlan, draft: Draft): PlanGoal | null {
  if (!plan.goal) return null;
  return draft.goal
    ? { amount: amountValue(draft.goal.text), currency: draft.goal.currency }
    : plan.goal;
}

/** Итог по черновику в сумах — как у сервера: поступления − расходы − цель. */
export function planTotals(plan: MonthPlan, draft: Draft): PlanTotals {
  const uzs = (amount: number, currency: string | null): number =>
    amount * (plan.rates[currency ?? ""] ?? 0);
  let spend = 0;
  let incomeRows = 0;
  for (const row of plan.rows) {
    const value = uzs(rowPlan(draft, row), row.currency);
    if (row.kind === "spend") spend += value;
    else incomeRows += value;
  }
  let incomes = 0;
  for (const event of shownEvents(plan, draft)) {
    if (event.name && event.amount !== null && event.amount > 0) {
      incomes += uzs(event.amount, event.currency);
    }
  }
  const shown = shownGoal(plan, draft);
  const goal = shown ? uzs(shown.amount, shown.currency) : 0;
  return { incomes, spend, goal, incomeRows, free: incomes - spend - goal };
}

function eventValue(value: EventDraft): EventValue {
  return {
    name: value.name,
    day: value.day,
    amount: `${value.income ? "" : "-"}${apiAmount(value.text)}`,
    currency: value.currency,
  };
}

export function toSave(draft: Draft): PlanSave {
  return {
    month: draft.month,
    rows: Object.entries(draft.rows).map(([key, edit]) => ({
      key,
      base: edit.base,
      plan: apiAmount(edit.text || "0"),
    })),
    events: Object.values(draft.events).map((edit) => ({
      slot: edit.base.slot,
      base: {
        name: edit.base.name,
        day: edit.base.day,
        amount: edit.base.amount,
        currency: edit.base.currency,
      },
      value: edit.value ? eventValue(edit.value) : null,
    })),
    goal: draft.goal
      ? {
          base: draft.goal.base,
          value: {
            amount: apiAmount(draft.goal.text || "0"),
            currency: draft.goal.currency,
          },
        }
      : null,
  };
}

/** Черновик поверх свежего плана: правки остаются, база — новые значения.
 * conflicts — где значение в таблице поменялось после начала правок. */
export function rebase(
  draft: Draft,
  fresh: MonthPlan,
): { draft: Draft; conflicts: string[] } {
  const conflicts: string[] = [];
  let next = emptyDraft(draft.month);
  for (const [key, edit] of Object.entries(draft.rows)) {
    const article = fresh.rows.find((row) => row.key === key && row.editable);
    if (!article) continue;
    if (!close(article.plan, edit.base)) conflicts.push(key);
    next = editRow(next, article, edit.text);
  }
  for (const [key, edit] of Object.entries(draft.events)) {
    const event = (fresh.events ?? []).find((item) => String(item.slot) === key);
    if (!event) continue;
    if (!sameCells(event, edit.base)) conflicts.push(`event:${key}`);
    next = editEvent(next, event, edit.value);
  }
  if (draft.goal && fresh.goal) {
    const { base } = draft.goal;
    if (
      base.currency !== fresh.goal.currency ||
      !close(base.amount, fresh.goal.amount)
    ) {
      conflicts.push("goal");
    }
    next = editGoal(next, fresh.goal, draft.goal.text, draft.goal.currency);
  }
  return { draft: next, conflicts };
}

/** Сумма для клавиатуры: копейки только у валюты; 0 — пусто. */
export function amountText(value: number, decimals: boolean): string {
  if (value <= 0) return "";
  if (!decimals) return String(Math.round(value));
  return value
    .toFixed(2)
    .replace(/\.?0+$/, "")
    .replace(".", ",");
}

function storageKey(userId: number, month: string): string {
  return `${STORAGE}:${userId}:${month}`;
}

function isDraft(value: unknown, month: string): value is Draft {
  return (
    typeof value === "object" &&
    value !== null &&
    "month" in value &&
    value.month === month &&
    "rows" in value &&
    typeof value.rows === "object" &&
    value.rows !== null &&
    "events" in value &&
    typeof value.events === "object" &&
    value.events !== null &&
    "goal" in value
  );
}

export function loadDraft(userId: number, month: string): Draft | null {
  try {
    const text = localStorage.getItem(storageKey(userId, month));
    if (!text) return null;
    const value: unknown = JSON.parse(text);
    return isDraft(value, month) && changeCount(value) > 0 ? value : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function storeDraft(userId: number, draft: Draft): void {
  try {
    const key = storageKey(userId, draft.month);
    if (changeCount(draft) === 0) localStorage.removeItem(key);
    else localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(draft));
  } catch {
    // память недоступна (приватный режим): черновик живёт до закрытия приложения
  }
}
```

```tsx
import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { PlanArticle, PlanEvent } from "../api";
import { planFixture } from "../mocks/fixtures";
import ArticleSheet from "./ArticleSheet";
import EventSheet from "./EventSheet";
import GoalSheet from "./GoalSheet";

function row(key: string): PlanArticle {
  const found = planFixture.rows.find((item) => item.key === key);
  if (!found) throw new Error(key);
  return found;
}

function slot(number: number): PlanEvent {
  const found = (planFixture.events ?? []).find((item) => item.slot === number);
  if (!found) throw new Error(String(number));
  return found;
}

describe("ArticleSheet", () => {
  it("shows the hints and fills the amount from them", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const onDone = vi.fn();
    render(
      <ArticleSheet
        article={row("кофе|UZS")}
        text=""
        history={planFixture.history}
        onDone={onDone}
        onClose={vi.fn()}
      />,
    );
    expect(screen.getByText("Среднее за июль–сентябрь")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("Октябрь пока (10 дней)")).toBeTruthy();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "= среднему" }));
    expect(screen.getByRole("status", { name: "Сумма" }).textContent).toBe(
      "1 800 000 UZS",
    );
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "= сентябрю" }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Стереть" }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Готово" }));
    expect(onDone).toHaveBeenCalledWith("200000");
  });
});

describe("EventSheet", () => {
  it("adds a payment only when it is filled in", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const onDone = vi.fn();
    render(
      <EventSheet
        event={slot(3)}
        currencies={["UZS", "USD"]}
        onDone={onDone}
        onClose={vi.fn()}
      />,
    );
    const done = screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Готово" });
    expect(done).toHaveProperty("disabled", true);
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Название"), "Садик");
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("День"), "5");
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("radio", { name: "Платёж" }));
    for (const key of ["4", "000", "000"]) {
      await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: key }));
    }
    await user.click(done);
    expect(onDone).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      name: "Садик",
      day: 5,
      income: false,
      text: "4000000",
      currency: "UZS",
    });
  });

  it("clears a filled row", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const onDone = vi.fn();
    render(
      <EventSheet
        event={slot(0)}
        currencies={["UZS"]}
        onDone={onDone}
        onClose={vi.fn()}
      />,
    );
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Название")).toHaveProperty("value", "Аванс");
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Удалить строку" }));
    expect(onDone).toHaveBeenCalledWith(null);
  });
});

describe("GoalSheet", () => {
  it("returns the amount and the currency", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const onDone = vi.fn();
    render(
      <GoalSheet
        goal={{ amount: 1_000_000, currency: "UZS" }}
        currencies={["UZS", "USD"]}
        onDone={onDone}
        onClose={vi.fn()}
      />,
    );
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "000" }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Готово" }));
    expect(onDone).toHaveBeenCalledWith("1000000000", "UZS");
  });
});
```
